 ## Issue 7: DBOS-on-PGLite launch — four required hooks
 
 **Status**: Mitigated (workarounds applied; revisit if DBOS upstream changes)
 **Discovered**: 2026-05-10 during 012-codeconv-runner Phase 6
 **Affects**: `codeconv migrate` (DBOS `dbos.launch()`) against the unified `.pgdb/` bridge
 
 ### Summary
 
 A fresh DBOS launch against the PGLite bridge requires four non-default configuration choices. Removing any one of them produces a different failure mode (separate-DB connect failure, deadlock on advisory lock, SQL syntax error at line 5, or hang on LISTEN/NOTIFY). See `codeconv/src/codeconv/db/engine.py::setup_dbos` and `codeconv/src/codeconv/_vendor/dbos_pglite_patch.py`.
 
 ### The four hooks
 
 1. **DB-name override**: `DBOSConfig(application_database_url=url, system_database_url=url, dbos_system_schema="dbos")`. DBOS defaults to a sibling `<dbname>_dbos_sys` database that PGLite cannot create; point both roles at `postgres` and isolate via the `dbos` schema (FR-015).
 2. **Pool sizing**: `db_engine_kwargs["pool_size"]=5, max_overflow=5` AND `sys_db_pool_size=5`. The vendored `pglite_engine_kwargs` defaults to `pool_size=1`, which deadlocks DBOS's `run_migrations` (holds one connection across an inner `ensure_dbos_schema` call). PGLite serialisation is preserved client-side because the bridge's `globalWorkChain` queues queries.
 3. **uuid-ossp rewrite preserves semicolon**: the SQLAlchemy `before_cursor_execute` filter substitutes `CREATE EXTENSION "uuid-ossp"` → `SELECT 1;` (with terminating `;`). Without the semicolon, the next CREATE TABLE concatenates in DBOS's multi-statement migration text and PGLite errors at line 5.
 4. **Disable LISTEN/NOTIFY**: `use_listen_notify=False` in `DBOSConfig`. PGLite does not implement `NOTIFY` end-to-end; leaving DBOS to poll triggers mystery hangs.
 
 ### PGLite specifics worth knowing
 
 - `SELECT current_database()` returns `'template1'` regardless of the requested `dbname` in the connection URL. PGLite routes everything to template1. Functionally fine — do not "fix" by changing the URL.
 - `pg_try_advisory_lock(...)` works and returns BOOLEAN. The earlier "cannot unpack non-iterable NoneType object" trace was a bug in the SQLAlchemy `before_cursor_execute` filter (must always return a tuple under `retval=True`), not a PGLite limitation.
 
 ### Where this is enforced
 
 - `codeconv/src/codeconv/db/engine.py::setup_dbos` — hooks 1, 2, 4.
 - `codeconv/src/codeconv/_vendor/dbos_pglite_patch.py::_install_sqlalchemy_uuid_ossp_filter` — hook 3.
 - `codeconv/tests/test_engine.py::test_apply_to_engine_installed` — end-to-end smoke (codeconv side).
 
 ### Why this isn't fixed upstream
 
 PGLite is feature 011's pre-req pattern; we treat it as a black-box dependency. The five workarounds above live in `codeconv/_vendor/dbos_pglite_patch.py` and `codeconv/db/engine.py` so a future DBOS or PGLite upgrade that lifts these limitations can drop them without touching consumer code.
 
 ---
 
 ## Issue 8: PGLite cluster files cannot live on exFAT
 
 **Status**: Mitigated via `--data-dir` override (release `v2026.05.11-2`)
 **Discovered**: 2026-05-11 when the freshly-merged `codeconv` was first invoked against the live repo
 **Affects**: Any `codeconv` (or other unified-bridge consumer) invocation where the repo lives on an exFAT volume
 
 ### Summary
 
 PGLite's WASM data files rely on POSIX-style file operations (atomic rename, advisory locks, certain mmap operations) that exFAT does not implement. When `.pgdb/` is created on exFAT, the bridge process crashes mid-DBOS-migration (typically around migration 4) — the client sees `psycopg.OperationalError: consuming input failed: server closed the connection unexpectedly` on a downstream query, and the bridge log is empty because the bridge died before flushing.
 
 This is environment-dependent and does NOT show up in `pytest` runs, because pytest's `tmp_path` lives under `%TEMP%` on `C:` (NTFS). The bug surfaces only against the live repo on `D:\BSTDEV\research\GLP\GLPNET\` which is on a `Lexar`-labelled exFAT drive (`Get-Volume D` shows `FileSystem : exFAT`).
 
 ### Fix
 
 Added a global `--data-dir <path>` flag to `codeconv` (cli.py / bridge_client.acquire_or_discover / workflow.run_discover). The flag decouples the PGLite cluster location from `--repo-root`. Point it at an NTFS directory:
 
 ```powershell
 codeconv --data-dir $env:LOCALAPPDATA/codeconv-pgdb migrate
 codeconv --data-dir $env:LOCALAPPDATA/codeconv-pgdb discover run
 ```
 
 Sidecar (`<data-dir>/bridge.json`), OS lock (`<data-dir>.bridge.lock/`), consumer registrations (`<data-dir>.consumers/`), and force-shutdown marker (`<data-dir>/.shutdown`) all follow the override automatically.
 
 ### Detection
 
 ```powershell
 Get-Volume <drive-letter> | Format-List FileSystem
 ```
 
 If `FileSystem` is `exFAT`, `FAT32`, or anything other than `NTFS` / `ReFS`, you MUST use `--data-dir` to point the cluster at an NTFS path. The repo source files themselves are fine on exFAT.
 
 ### Why this isn't a code fix
 
 PGLite is a WASM build of upstream PostgreSQL. It assumes a POSIX-class filesystem under the data directory. We cannot fix this in `codeconv` itself — only route around it. The flag and this doc-note are the mitigation.
+
+---
+
+## Issue 9: live shared cluster is PG16; 0.4.5 bridge is PG17 (not forward-compatible)
+
+**Status**: Worked around for feature 015 via a separate side cluster (option c); the shared-cluster migration is gated/pending
+**Discovered**: 2026-05-16 completing feature 015's live-cluster tasks (T039/T040/T043)
+**Affects**: Any live-cluster `codeconv` work on a branch whose bridge is PGLite ≥0.3.0
+
+### Summary
+
+The canonical shared cluster `C:/pglite/research/glpnet/` (`PG_VERSION`=16) was created by old PGLite 0.2.x. The PGLite-0.4.5 upgrade (aborted-txn fix) embeds PostgreSQL 17. PGLite data dirs are **not** forward-compatible (0.2.x=PG16, 0.3.0+=PG17). That cluster also holds D2NET's `public` schema, so an in-place dump/restore is a gated cross-tool op (runbook: `docs/pglite-0.4-cluster-migration-runbook.md`, requires Gabi's go + D2NET sign-off, not yet executed).
+
+### Workaround (used for feature 015)
+
+Stand up a separate fresh PG17 cluster at a side path (e.g. `C:/pglite/research/glpnet-015/`), `codeconv --data-dir <side> migrate` then `discover run`, and run all live tasks there. D2NET's shared cluster is left untouched until D2NET drives its own migration.
+
+### Doc staleness noticed (not yet fixed in spec/quickstart)
+
+`specs/015-codeconv-depgraph/quickstart.md` Flow H still uses `--data-dir .pgdb` and reads `.pgdb\bridge.json`; the bridge sidecar is codeconv-managed and ephemeral (no fixed `bridge.json` for arbitrary data-dirs — discover via `bridge_client.acquire_or_discover`). The cycle-fixture check references `$json.cycle_count` but the key is `$json.metadata.cycle_count`. These are doc bugs, not product bugs — the bridge/CLI are correct.

 # Contract: `/codeconv-discover` (and `codeconv discover`)
 
 Source: spec FR-018, FR-019, FR-020, FR-021, FR-022, FR-023, FR-024, FR-025; clarifications Q7, Q9, Q12, Q13, Q14, Q15; research R7, R11, R12, R15.
 
 ## Invocation
 
 Slash form: `/codeconv-discover [flags]` → CLI form: `codeconv discover [flags]`.
 
 ## Flags
 
 | Flag | Type | Default | Semantics |
 |---|---|---|---|
 | `--from-tombstones` | bool | false | Reconstruct inventory from `.codeconv/tombstones/` only. No `.dart` source is read (FR-022; no-source guarantee scoped to this mode). Mutually exclusive with `--verify-tombstones`. |
 | `--verify-tombstones` | bool | false | Read-only source-truth audit (feature-015 reconciliation). Reads `.dart` sources; NO DB writes, NO tombstone rewrites; bridge NOT required (delayed/skipped acquisition). Mutually exclusive with `--from-tombstones`. |
 | `--root <path>` | path | `glp_runtime_net` (relative to repo root) | Override the discover subtree. Default matches FR-018; override only for testing. |
 | `--quiet` | bool | false | Suppress per-file logging. |
 | `--json` | bool | false | Emit JSON summary instead of human-readable. |
 | `--dry-run` | bool | false | Walk, parse, compute would-be writes; do NOT touch DB or tombstones. Useful for CI. |
 | `--no-orphan-revival` | bool | false | Skip the FR-025 revival step (testing only; default behavior is to revive). |
 
 ## Subtree scope (FR-018)
 
 Discover walks `<root>/**/*.dart`. It MUST exclude:
 
 - Any path containing a `.dart_tool/` segment.
 - Any path containing a `build/` segment.
 - Any file matching `*.g.dart`, `*.freezed.dart`, `*.gen.dart`.
 - Symlinks pointing outside the subtree (treated as outside; not followed).
 
 It MUST NOT process any `.dart` file outside `<root>` even if reachable through symlinks or relative imports.
 
 ## Steps (normal mode)
 
 ```
 1. Acquire bridge endpoint (codeconv runner startup).
 2. Open or create discover_runs row (mode='normal').
 3. Walker phase:
    - Recursively enumerate `.dart` files under <root> with the exclusion rules.
    - Compute total file count → discover_runs.files_total.
 4. Per-file DBOS step (workflow checkpoint per file):
    - Compute mtime + sha256.
    - Idempotence short-circuit: if dart_files row exists with matching (mtime, sha256), bump files_skipped_idempotent and continue.
    - Read leading doc-comment block (R11).
    - Parse import directives, resolve, filter to inside-subtree only (R12).
    - UPSERT codeconv.dart_files row.
    - Replace dart_imports rows for from_path = this file with the deduplicated set.
    - Replace dart_callers rows for to_path = this file BY recomputing from dart_imports.
      (Note: dart_callers is consistent only after all per-file steps complete; the per-file step
      writes the rows it KNOWS about; a final phase 5 reconciles.)
    - Write tombstone .codeconv/tombstones/<rel>.dart.md.
    - Bump files_processed.
 5. Reconciliation phase (single DBOS step):
-   - Recompute dart_callers from the now-complete dart_imports table (idempotent rewrite).
+   - REFERENTIAL COMPLETENESS (Amendment v3 — option A): drop any dart_imports
+     edge whose endpoint is not an inventoried dart_files path. This occurs when
+     an in-subtree import directive resolves (by path shape, R12) to a file that
+     does not exist on disk and was therefore never inventoried. Each dropped
+     edge is counted as a warning ("missing target: <to_path> referenced by
+     <from_path>"). This is the SAME accepted, warned divergence as the
+     `--from-tombstones` referential-completeness rule (§ Steps (`--from-tombstones`)
+     step 3) and the SC-007 caveat applies symmetrically. Rationale: feature 015's
+     `algorithm.compute` (`contracts/depgraph_algorithm.md` § Algorithm step 2,
+     test obligation 8) contractually raises `ValueError` on a dangling endpoint,
+     so a referentially-complete dart_imports is a normal-mode invariant, not
+     merely a from-tombstones one.
+   - Recompute dart_callers from the now-referentially-complete dart_imports table (idempotent rewrite).
    - Find files in dart_files that are no longer present on disk → orphan: move to dart_files_orphaned, move tombstone to .codeconv/tombstones/.orphaned/.
    - Find files in dart_files_orphaned that are present on disk again → revive (FR-025): move row back, move tombstone back, refresh mtime + sha256, recompute edges.
    - Detect imports BY files OUTSIDE the subtree pointing INTO the subtree (FR-023): emit warnings; do NOT record edges.
    - Detect duplicate import directives (FR-019): emit warnings.
 6. Mark discover_runs.completed_at = NOW(); summarise.
 ```
 
 ## Steps (`--from-tombstones` mode)
 
 ```
 1. Walk .codeconv/tombstones/**/*.dart.md (excluding .orphaned/) → tombstone set T.
 2. PARSE + STRUCTURAL-VALIDATE — BEFORE any bridge acquisition, before the
    discover_runs row, before any DB/migrate path (so a bad input leaves the
    inventory untouched):
    a. Parse every tombstone's YAML frontmatter (NOT the body). Any unparseable
       tombstone, OR a tombstone missing/!type-valid on a REQUIRED feature-012
       field → ABORT with an actionable stderr message naming the file(s);
       exit 65; ZERO inventory mutation. [hard-fail]
    b. An optional feature-015 key (topo_level, cycle_group_id, status,
       conversion_started_at, conversion_completed_at, target_path) that is
       present-but-type-invalid → record a warning and ingest the base
       inventory row anyway with that one field treated as null. [warn; the
       base 012 row is NEVER dropped for a bad optional 015 field]
 3. REFERENTIAL COMPLETENESS (still before any mutation): build the full path
    set of T. Any path appearing in some tombstone's `dependencies`/`callers`
    with no tombstone in T → the offending EDGE is dropped and counted as a
    warning ("missing tombstone: <path> referenced by <referrer>"). Documented
    SC-007 caveat: dropped dangling edges are an accepted, warned divergence;
    the dart_files/dart_imports/dart_callers content otherwise matches a
    normal-mode run.
 4. Acquire bridge endpoint; open discover_runs row (mode='from_tombstones').
 5. Reconcile in ONE transaction (atomic-per-run; a crash/error rolls the whole
    thing back — inventory never left half-wiped):
    - dart_imports, dart_callers: TRUNCATE then bulk-reinsert from T (these
      have no inbound FK).
    - dart_files: UPSERT each path from T (`INSERT … ON CONFLICT (path) DO
      UPDATE`), then DELETE only dart_files rows whose path is absent from T.
      dart_files is NOT blanket-TRUNCATEd, so feature-015's
      dart_conversions / dart_depgraph (FK → dart_files ON DELETE CASCADE) are
      PRESERVED for every surviving file. A path genuinely absent from T is a
      vanished file; its DELETE cascades its conversion/depgraph row away,
      which is correct. (Option B — chosen over a blanket TRUNCATE precisely
      so a from-tombstones inventory rebuild does not destroy conversion
      progress; depgraph is recomputed by `codeconv depgraph compute` anyway.)
 6. For each tombstone in .codeconv/tombstones/.orphaned/:
    - UPSERT codeconv.dart_files_orphaned row from frontmatter.
 7. Mark completed_at; summarise (warnings include type-invalid-015-field and
    dropped-dangling-edge entries).
 
 NO `.dart` source is read in `--from-tombstones` mode (FR-022; this no-source
 guarantee is scoped to THIS mode only — contrast `--verify-tombstones` below).
 The rebuilt dart_files / dart_imports / dart_callers MUST equal what a
 normal-mode run on the same source state would produce (SC-007), modulo the
 documented dropped-dangling-edge warnings. dart_conversions / dart_depgraph are
 out of SC-007 scope and are preserved across the rebuild, not recomputed by
 discover.
 ```
 
 ## Steps (`--verify-tombstones` mode)
 
 `codeconv discover --verify-tombstones` is a READ-ONLY source-truth audit added
 for the feature-015 reconciliation. It DOES read `.dart` sources (this is the
 operation that needs source truth; the FR-022 "no `.dart`" guarantee applies
 only to `--from-tombstones`, never here). NO DB writes, NO tombstone rewrites.
 The bridge is NOT required: bridge acquisition is delayed past mode dispatch and
 skipped entirely for this flag (a CLI change from "acquire before dispatch").
 Mutually exclusive with `--from-tombstones`.
 
 ```
 1. Walk .codeconv/tombstones/**/*.dart.md, SKIPPING .codeconv/tombstones/.orphaned/
    (intentional orphans must not be reported as "missing source"). → set T.
 2. Parse every tombstone's YAML. Any unparseable / format-invalid tombstone →
    abort, exit 65 (same code + now-broadened table wording as
    `--from-tombstones`).
 3. Build the FULL in-subtree import graph ONCE by scanning all present `.dart`
    sources (callers are the inverse of the whole import graph — they cannot be
    verified per-file in isolation), then for each tombstone whose `.dart`
    source is present: recompute sha256 and compare recorded sha256 + the
    tombstone's `dependencies`/`callers` against the freshly-derived values.
    Any mismatch → warning ("stale tombstone: <path> differs from source

 ## Phase 4: User Story 2 — Advance the conversion frontier (Priority: P2)
 
 **Goal**: `mark-started` and `mark-completed` subcommands populate `codeconv.dart_conversions` and update the corresponding tombstone YAML. A subsequent `compute` re-derives `status` correctly: `pending` → `ready` (when all deps converted) → `in_progress` (after `mark-started`) → `converted` (after `mark-completed`). The four spec US2 acceptance scenarios pass end-to-end.
 
 **Independent Test**: After at least one file has been marked through both phases (`mark-started` → `mark-completed`), re-run `compute`. Spec US2 acceptance scenarios 1–4 verified per `quickstart.md` Flow H step 6.

 ## Phase 5: User Story 3 — Convert a cycle as a single batch (Priority: P2)
 
 **Goal**: When the import graph contains a multi-file SCC (cycle), all members share the same `cycle_group_id` and `topo_level`, and become `status='ready'` together when their SCC-external dependencies are all converted. A developer can `mark-started` all SCC members, convert them as a batch, then `mark-completed` all members; downstream files then advance correctly.
 
 **Independent Test**: Construct a 3-file cycle fixture (A→B→C→A, plus D→A). Run `compute`. Verify: (a) A, B, C share one `cycle_group_id` and one `topo_level`; (b) D has a higher `topo_level` and a distinct `cycle_group_id`; (c) A/B/C all have `status='ready'` simultaneously; (d) D has `status='pending'`. After `mark-completed` on all of A, B, C: D advances to `status='ready'`. Spec US3 acceptance scenarios 1–3 verified per `quickstart.md` § "US3 — 3-file cycle fixture".
 
 ### Tests for User Story 3 (REQUIRED — write FIRST, ensure they FAIL before implementation)
 
 - [x] T027 [P] [US3] Create `codeconv/tests/test_depgraph_cycle_fixture.py` with: `test_three_file_cycle_shares_one_cycle_group_id`, `test_three_file_cycle_shares_one_topo_level`, `test_cycle_count_metric_equals_one_for_3cycle`, `test_d_above_cycle_at_higher_topo_level`, `test_d_becomes_ready_when_all_cycle_members_converted`, `test_mark_started_on_one_cycle_member_keeps_others_ready` per spec US3 acceptance scenarios 1–3. Gate with `@needs_bridge`
 - [x] T028 [P] [US3] Add the synthetic cycle fixture under `specs/015-codeconv-depgraph/scripts/cycle_fixture/`: three minimal `.dart` files A.dart, B.dart, C.dart in a 3-cycle, plus D.dart depending on A. Each file is ≤10 lines of Dart with the relevant `import` directives. Include a `pubspec.yaml` if discover requires one (verify against feature 014's pubspec rewrite — likely yes)
 
 ### Implementation for User Story 3
 
 (US3 reuses US1's algorithm — no new algorithm code. SCC handling is built into Tarjan from T013. US3's only new code is the cycle fixture and the dedicated test surface.)

 ---
 
 ## Dependencies & Execution Order
 
 ### Phase Dependencies
 
 - **Setup (Phase 1)**: No dependencies — can start immediately.
 - **Foundational (Phase 2)**: Depends on Setup. BLOCKS Phases 3–6 (all user stories need the new tables).
 - **US1 (Phase 3)**: Depends on Foundational completion. Internally: tests (T010–T012) before implementation (T013–T016); validation (T017–T018) after implementation.
 - **US2 (Phase 4)**: Depends on US1 (T013 algorithm + T015 workflow.run_compute) because mark-* + status derivation reuses them.
 - **US3 (Phase 5)**: Depends on US1 (Tarjan correctness on cycles is established in T013/T010 item 3). US3 reuses everything; only adds the cycle fixture and dedicated tests.
 - **Phase 6 (slash skill + stamp + rebuild)**: Depends on US1, US2, US3 (stamp-tombstones needs all five YAML keys present; rebuild-conversions needs the mark-* round-trip).
 - **Phase 7 (polish)**: Depends on Phase 6 complete. T043 (tombstone refresh) runs LAST so it captures both depgraph and conversion state for every file.
 
 ### Within Each User Story
 
 - **TDD ordering**: tests in T010–T012, T019–T020, T027, T032–T033 are written FIRST and MUST FAIL against the unmodified codebase before implementation tasks (T013–T018, T021–T026, T029–T031, T034–T037) are touched.
 - **Implementation ordering within US1**: T013 (algorithm) and T014 (json_writer) are independent and can be done in parallel. T015 (workflow.run_compute) depends on both. T016 (Typer app) depends on T015.
 - **Implementation ordering within US2**: T022 (extend _FIELD_ORDER) is independent. T021 (tombstone_writer.py) depends on T022. T023 (workflow.run_mark_*) depends on T021. T024 (Typer subcommands) depends on T023. T025 (status derivation in run_compute) is independent of T021–T024 but touches the same file as T015.
 - **Implementation ordering within US3**: T028 (fixture) is independent. T029–T030 are verifications, not new code.
 - **Implementation ordering within Phase 6**: T034–T035 (workflow functions) depend on US2's tombstone_writer.py and feature-012's tombstone.py. T036 wires them into the Typer app. T037 (skill) is independent of all the Python code.
 
 ### Parallel Opportunities
 
 - All Phase 1 setup tasks (T001–T005) can run in parallel — different artefacts, no shared state.
 - T010, T011, T012 (US1 tests) are different files → parallel.
 - T013, T014 (algorithm + json_writer) are different files → parallel.
 - T019, T020 (US2 tests) — T019 is its own file (test_depgraph_mark.py); T020 appends to test_depgraph_compute.py created in T011. T019 is [P] with most US2 work; T020 conflicts with US1's T011 (same file) so must be sequenced after T011 completes.
 - T032, T033 (Phase 6 tests) are different files → parallel.
 - T041, T042, T044 in Phase 7 — different concerns, different files → parallel.
 
 ---
 
 ## Parallel Example: User Story 1 tests
 
 ```bash
 # Launch all US1 tests together (different files, can run in parallel):
 Task: "Create codeconv/tests/test_depgraph_algorithm.py with 8 pure-stdlib unit tests per contracts/depgraph_algorithm.md"
 Task: "Create codeconv/tests/test_depgraph_compute.py with 4 @needs_bridge tests per contracts/depgraph_cli.md"
 Task: "Create codeconv/tests/test_depgraph_idempotence.py with 2 @needs_bridge tests per SC-002 / SC-008"
 
 # Then in implementation:
 Task: "Create codeconv/src/codeconv/tools/depgraph/algorithm.py per contracts/depgraph_algorithm.md"
 Task: "Create codeconv/src/codeconv/tools/depgraph/json_writer.py per contracts/depgraph_cli.md JSON output shape"
 ```
 
 ---
 
 ## Implementation Strategy
 
 ### MVP First (User Story 1 Only)
 
 1. Phase 1: Setup baseline (T001–T005).
 2. Phase 2: Migration + schema isolation test (T006–T009).
 3. Phase 3: US1 (T010–T018).
 4. **STOP and VALIDATE**: SC-001 (warm < 5 s), SC-002 (idempotence), SC-003 (edge invariant), SC-005 (`ready` array readable), SC-007 (schema isolation), SC-008 (dry-run no-op) verified by hand running `quickstart.md` Flow H steps 0–8 (skipping steps 6 / 9 / 10 which require US2/Phase 6).
 5. If only US1 is shipped (US2 deferred), the feature still delivers the headline value: a developer reads the `ready` array and starts converting leaves. They cannot mark progress (mark-* not yet shipped), but the ordering is complete and authoritative.
 
 ### Full Delivery
 
 1. US1 → US2 → US3 → Phase 6 → Phase 7, sequential per the Phase Dependencies above.
 2. T043's tombstone refresh runs LAST — it captures depgraph + conversion state for every file in one commit.
 3. PR contains a small number of logical commits:
    - Migration commit (`codeconv/src/codeconv/db/migrations/versions/0002_dart_depgraph.py`)
    - Algorithm + tests commit (`codeconv/src/codeconv/tools/depgraph/algorithm.py`, `test_depgraph_algorithm.py`)
    - Workflow + CLI commit (`workflow.py`, `__init__.py`, `json_writer.py`, plus `test_depgraph_compute.py`, `test_depgraph_idempotence.py`)
    - Mark + tombstone-writer commit (US2: `tombstone_writer.py`, `_FIELD_ORDER` extension, `run_mark_*`, plus `test_depgraph_mark.py`)
    - Cycle fixture + tests commit (US3: `cycle_fixture/`, `test_depgraph_cycle_fixture.py`)
    - Stamp + rebuild commit (Phase 6: `run_stamp_tombstones`, `run_rebuild_conversions_from_tombstones`, plus tests + the `SKILL.md`)
    - Tombstone refresh commit (T043, `.codeconv/tombstones/` only)
    - `.gitignore` update commit (T042)
 4. Single PR onto `main` per `docs/BRANCHING.md`. Same-day CalVer tag minted on merge per `docs/VERSIONING.md`.

1:"""codeconv discover workflow — Phase 6 / US4 / T072.
2:
3:Maps to ``specs/012-codeconv-runner/contracts/codeconv_discover_cli.md``
4:§ Steps (normal mode) and § Steps (--from-tombstones mode).
5:
6:High-level entry point: :func:`run_discover`. The CLI surface in
7:``__init__.py`` calls this function; tests call it via subprocess.
8:
9:Effective workflow durability is provided by:
10:
11:1. Per-file ``(mtime, sha256)`` idempotence short-circuit (R15) — files
12:   already inventoried are skipped on re-run, so killing mid-flight and
13:   re-invoking the same command resumes correctly (SC-009 / FR-017).
14:2. UPSERT semantics on ``codeconv.dart_files`` so partial writes can be
15:   safely retried.
16:
17:DBOS-managed workflow wrapping (``@DBOS.workflow`` / ``@DBOS.step``) is
18:deferred to Phase 7 polish; the spec FR-017 behavioural contract — kill
19:and resume yields no re-parse of completed files — is satisfied by the
20:short-circuit mechanism above. The deferral is captured in the polish
21:task list.

38:from codeconv.bridge_client import acquire_or_discover
39:from codeconv.db.engine import build_engine

57:def register(dbos_app: Any) -> None:
58:    """Register discover with DBOS at runner startup.
59:
60:    Currently a no-op — see module docstring on FR-017 deferral. Kept on
61:    the public surface so the contract (``codeconv_tool_contract.md``)
62:    is satisfied and a future polish phase can wire up the real DBOS
63:    workflow without an API change.
64:    """
65:    return None
66:
67:
68:def run_discover(
69:    repo_root: Path,
70:    *,
71:    mode: str = "normal",
72:    root: Optional[Path] = None,
73:    dry_run: bool = False,
74:    no_orphan_revival: bool = False,
75:    quiet: bool = True,
76:    bridge_script: Optional[Path] = None,
77:    data_dir: Optional[Path] = None,
78:) -> dict:
79:    """Dispatch on ``mode`` → run discover workflow → return summary dict.
80:
81:    Per ``specs/012-codeconv-runner/contracts/codeconv_discover_cli.md``
82:    (Amendment v2), mode dispatch happens BEFORE bridge acquisition:
83:
84:    - ``verify_tombstones``: read-only source-truth audit; the bridge is
85:      NOT required and is never acquired.
86:    - ``from_tombstones``: parse + structural-validate + referential-
87:      completeness PREFLIGHT runs before any bridge / ``discover_runs`` /
88:      DB touch, so a bad input leaves the inventory untouched (exit 65).
89:    - ``normal``: unchanged feature-012/-014 behaviour.
90:
91:    Every returned summary carries an ``exit_code`` (0 on success);
92:    ``data_dir`` overrides the default ``<repo_root>/.pgdb`` cluster
93:    location (for repos on PGLite-hostile filesystems like exFAT).
94:    """
95:    repo_root = Path(repo_root).resolve()
96:    subtree = (root or (repo_root / "glp_runtime_net")).resolve()
97:    tombstones_root = repo_root / ".codeconv" / "tombstones"
98:    # `--verify-tombstones` is strictly read-only (contract: NO DB writes,
99:    # NO tombstone rewrites). Creating the tombstone dir in a clean
100:    # checkout would be a filesystem write — only normal / from_tombstones
101:    # (which may write/rebuild tombstones) create it.
102:    if mode != "verify_tombstones":
103:        tombstones_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
104:
105:    def _finalise(summary: dict) -> dict:
106:        summary["mode"] = mode
107:        try:
108:            summary["root"] = str(
109:                subtree.relative_to(repo_root)
110:            ).replace("\\", "/")
111:        except ValueError:
112:            summary["root"] = str(subtree)
113:        summary.setdefault("exit_code", 0)
114:        return summary
115:
116:    # ---- verify-tombstones: NO bridge, read-only, reads .dart -----------
117:    if mode == "verify_tombstones":
118:        return _finalise(
119:            _run_verify_tombstones(repo_root, subtree, tombstones_root)
120:        )
121:
122:    # ---- from-tombstones: PREFLIGHT before any bridge / DB --------------
123:    preflight: Optional[dict] = None
124:    if mode == "from_tombstones":
125:        preflight = _preflight_from_tombstones(tombstones_root)
126:        if preflight.get("abort"):
127:            return _finalise(
128:                {
129:                    "files_walked": 0,
130:                    "files_processed": 0,
131:                    "files_skipped_idempotent": 0,
132:                    "imports": 0,
133:                    "callers": 0,
134:                    "orphaned": 0,
135:                    "revived": 0,
136:                    "warnings": preflight["warnings"],
137:                    "duration_seconds": preflight["duration_seconds"],
138:                    "error": preflight.get("error"),
139:                    "exit_code": 65,
140:                }
141:            )
142:        if dry_run:
143:            # --dry-run: do NOT touch DB / bridge; report would-be counts
144:            # straight from the validated preflight.
145:            return _finalise(
146:                {
147:                    "files_walked": len(preflight["files"]),
148:                    "files_processed": len(preflight["files"]),
149:                    "files_skipped_idempotent": 0,
150:                    "imports": len(preflight["imports"]),
151:                    "callers": len(preflight["callers"]),
152:                    "orphaned": len(preflight["orphaned"]),
153:                    "revived": 0,
154:                    "warnings": preflight["warnings"],
155:                    "duration_seconds": preflight["duration_seconds"],
156:                    "exit_code": 0,
157:                }
158:            )
159:
160:    started_at = _utc_now()
161:    run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
162:
163:    package_name = None
164:    pubspec_warning = None
165:    if mode == "normal":
166:        # Feature 014 / FR-004: read pubspec.yaml once per run; cache the
167:        # (name, warning) pair and thread it through the parser. Only
168:        # normal mode parses .dart, so this is normal-mode-only.
169:        package_name, pubspec_warning = read_package_name(
170:            subtree, repo_root=repo_root
171:        )
172:
173:    endpoint = acquire_or_discover(
174:        repo_root,
175:        ready_timeout=30.0,
176:        bridge_script=bridge_script,
177:        data_dir=data_dir,
178:    )
179:    engine = build_engine(endpoint)
180:
181:    with engine.begin() as conn:
182:        conn.execute(
183:            text(
184:                "INSERT INTO codeconv.discover_runs "
185:                "  (id, started_at, mode, files_processed, files_skipped_idempotent, warnings) "
186:                "VALUES (:id, :started_at, :mode, 0, 0, '[]'::jsonb)"
187:            ),
188:            {"id": run_id, "started_at": started_at, "mode": mode},
189:        )
190:
191:    try:
192:        if mode == "from_tombstones":
193:            assert preflight is not None  # validated above
194:            summary = _run_from_tombstones(
195:                engine, run_id, preflight, dry_run
196:            )
197:        else:
198:            summary = _run_normal(
199:                engine,
200:                run_id,
201:                repo_root,
202:                subtree,
203:                tombstones_root,
204:                dry_run,
205:                no_orphan_revival,
206:                quiet,
207:                package_name=package_name,
208:                pubspec_warning=pubspec_warning,
209:            )
210:    finally:
211:        with engine.begin() as conn:
212:            conn.execute(
213:                text(
214:                    "UPDATE codeconv.discover_runs SET completed_at = :t WHERE id = :id"
215:                ),
216:                {"t": _utc_now(), "id": run_id},
217:            )
218:
219:    return _finalise(summary)
220:
221:
222:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
223:# Normal mode
224:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
225:
226:
227:def _run_normal(
228:    engine: Engine,
229:    run_id: str,
230:    repo_root: Path,
231:    subtree: Path,
232:    tombstones_root: Path,
233:    dry_run: bool,
234:    no_orphan_revival: bool,
235:    quiet: bool,
236:    *,
237:    package_name: Optional[str] = None,
238:    pubspec_warning: Optional[dict] = None,
239:) -> dict:
240:    t0 = time.monotonic()
241:    warnings_list: list[dict] = []
242:
243:    # Feature 014 / FR-005: emit the pubspec_missing warning exactly once
244:    # per run, regardless of file count.
245:    if pubspec_warning is not None:
246:        warnings_list.append(pubspec_warning)
247:
248:    files = list(walk_dart_files(subtree))
249:    files_total = len(files)
250:
251:    with engine.begin() as conn:
252:        conn.execute(
253:            text(
254:                "UPDATE codeconv.discover_runs SET files_total = :n WHERE id = :id"
255:            ),
256:            {"n": files_total, "id": run_id},
257:        )
258:
259:    files_processed = 0
260:    files_skipped = 0
261:    for abs_path, rel_path in files:
262:        result = _process_one_file(
263:            engine,
264:            run_id,
265:            abs_path,
266:            rel_path,
267:            subtree,
268:            tombstones_root,
269:            dry_run,
270:            warnings_list,
271:            package_name=package_name,
272:        )
273:        if result == "processed":
274:            files_processed += 1
275:        elif result == "skipped":
276:            files_skipped += 1
277:
278:    orphaned = 0
279:    revived = 0
280:    if not dry_run:
281:        # Reconciliation phase.
282:        with engine.begin() as conn:
283:            # Referential completeness (Amendment v3 / option A): an
284:            # in-subtree import directive can resolve by path shape (R12)
285:            # to a file that does not exist on disk and was therefore
286:            # never inventoried into dart_files. Such an edge is dangling.
287:            # feature-015 algorithm.compute contractually raises on a
288:            # dangling endpoint, so a referentially-complete dart_imports
289:            # is a normal-mode invariant — same accepted, warned
290:            # divergence as the --from-tombstones rule (SC-007 caveat).
291:            valid_paths = {
292:                r[0]
293:                for r in conn.execute(
294:                    text("SELECT path FROM codeconv.dart_files")
295:                ).all()
296:            }
297:            has_dangling = False
298:            for from_path, to_path in conn.execute(
299:                text(
300:                    "SELECT from_path, to_path FROM codeconv.dart_imports"
301:                )
302:            ).all():
303:                if to_path not in valid_paths:
304:                    has_dangling = True
305:                    warnings_list.append(
306:                        {
307:                            "kind": "missing_target",
308:                            "path": to_path,
309:                            "referrer": from_path,
310:                        }
311:                    )
312:                elif from_path not in valid_paths:
313:                    has_dangling = True
314:                    warnings_list.append(
315:                        {
316:                            "kind": "missing_target",
317:                            "path": from_path,
318:                            "referrer": to_path,
319:                        }
320:                    )
321:            if has_dangling:
322:                conn.execute(
323:                    text(
324:                        "DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_imports i "
325:                        "WHERE NOT EXISTS ("
326:                        "  SELECT 1 FROM codeconv.dart_files f "
327:                        "  WHERE f.path = i.to_path) "
328:                        "   OR NOT EXISTS ("
329:                        "  SELECT 1 FROM codeconv.dart_files f "
330:                        "  WHERE f.path = i.from_path)"
331:                    )
332:                )
333:            # Recompute callers from the now-referentially-complete imports.
334:            conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_callers"))
335:            conn.execute(
336:                text(
337:                    "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_callers (from_path, to_path) "
338:                    "SELECT from_path, to_path FROM codeconv.dart_imports "
339:                    "ON CONFLICT (from_path, to_path) DO NOTHING"
340:                )
341:            )
342:
343:        # Orphan files no longer present.
344:        present = {rel for _, rel in files}
345:        with engine.begin() as conn:
346:            rows = conn.execute(
347:                text("SELECT path FROM codeconv.dart_files")
348:            ).all()
349:            in_db = {r[0] for r in rows}
350:            absent = in_db - present
351:            for rel in sorted(absent):
352:                conn.execute(
353:                    text(
354:                        "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_files_orphaned "
355:                        "  (path, name, purpose, key_idea, mtime, sha256, "
356:                        "   discovered_at, orphaned_at) "
357:                        "SELECT path, name, purpose, key_idea, mtime, sha256, "
358:                        "       discovered_at, NOW() "
359:                        "FROM codeconv.dart_files WHERE path = :p "
360:                        "ON CONFLICT (path) DO UPDATE SET "
361:                        "  purpose = EXCLUDED.purpose, "
362:                        "  key_idea = EXCLUDED.key_idea, "
363:                        "  mtime = EXCLUDED.mtime, "
364:                        "  sha256 = EXCLUDED.sha256, "
365:                        "  orphaned_at = NOW()"
366:                    ),
367:                    {"p": rel},
368:                )
369:                conn.execute(
370:                    text("DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_files WHERE path = :p"),
371:                    {"p": rel},
372:                )
373:                conn.execute(
374:                    text(
375:                        "DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_imports "
376:                        "WHERE from_path = :p OR to_path = :p"
377:                    ),
378:                    {"p": rel},
379:                )
380:                conn.execute(
381:                    text(
382:                        "DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_callers "
383:                        "WHERE from_path = :p OR to_path = :p"
384:                    ),
385:                    {"p": rel},
386:                )
387:                move_to_orphaned(tombstones_root, rel)
388:                orphaned += 1
389:
390:        # Revive previously-orphaned files now present.
391:        if not no_orphan_revival:
392:            with engine.begin() as conn:
393:                rows = conn.execute(
394:                    text("SELECT path FROM codeconv.dart_files_orphaned")
395:                ).all()
396:                orphaned_set = {r[0] for r in rows}
397:            for rel in sorted(orphaned_set & present):
398:                with engine.begin() as conn:
399:                    conn.execute(
400:                        text(
401:                            "DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_files_orphaned WHERE path = :p"
402:                        ),
403:                        {"p": rel},
404:                    )
405:                move_from_orphaned(tombstones_root, rel)
406:                revived += 1
407:
408:        # Outside-subtree caller scan (FR-023 + Feature 014 / FR-006).
409:        warnings_list.extend(
410:            _scan_outside_callers(
411:                repo_root, subtree, package_name=package_name
412:            )
413:        )
414:
415:        # Backfill tombstone callers list now that the inverted graph is settled.
416:        _backfill_tombstone_callers(engine, tombstones_root)
417:
418:    with engine.begin() as conn:
419:        imports_count = (
420:            conn.execute(
421:                text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM codeconv.dart_imports")
422:            ).scalar()
423:            or 0
424:        )
425:        callers_count = (
426:            conn.execute(
427:                text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM codeconv.dart_callers")
428:            ).scalar()
429:            or 0
430:        )
431:
432:    duration = time.monotonic() - t0
433:    return {
434:        "files_walked": files_total,
435:        "files_processed": files_processed,
436:        "files_skipped_idempotent": files_skipped,
437:        "imports": int(imports_count),
438:        "callers": int(callers_count),
439:        "orphaned": orphaned,
440:        "revived": revived,
441:        "warnings": warnings_list,
442:        "duration_seconds": round(duration, 2),
443:    }
444:
445:
446:def _process_one_file(
447:    engine: Engine,
448:    run_id: str,
449:    abs_path: Path,
450:    rel_path: str,
451:    subtree: Path,
452:    tombstones_root: Path,
453:    dry_run: bool,
454:    warnings_list: list[dict],
455:    *,
456:    package_name: Optional[str] = None,
457:) -> str:
458:    try:
459:        st = abs_path.stat()
460:    except OSError:
461:        return "skipped"
462:    mtime = datetime.fromtimestamp(st.st_mtime, tz=timezone.utc)
463:
464:    try:
465:        sha256 = hashlib.sha256(abs_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
466:    except OSError:
467:        return "skipped"
468:
469:    # Idempotence short-circuit (R15) — primary resume mechanism.
470:    with engine.begin() as conn:
471:        existing = conn.execute(
472:            text("SELECT sha256 FROM codeconv.dart_files WHERE path = :p"),
473:            {"p": rel_path},
474:        ).first()
475:    if existing is not None and existing[0] == sha256:
476:        return "skipped"
477:
478:    if dry_run:
479:        return "processed"
480:
481:    purpose = extract_leading_doc(abs_path)
482:    key_idea = purpose
483:
484:    with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as caught:
485:        warnings.simplefilter("always")
486:        imports_list = extract_imports(abs_path, subtree, package_name)
487:    for w in caught:
488:        msg = str(w.message)
489:        if "duplicate import" in msg:
490:            warnings_list.append(
491:                {
492:                    "kind": "duplicate_import",
493:                    "file": rel_path,
494:                    "import": msg,
495:                }
496:            )
497:
498:    name = abs_path.name
499:    with engine.begin() as conn:
500:        conn.execute(
501:            text(
502:                "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_files "
503:                "  (path, name, purpose, key_idea, mtime, sha256, discovered_at) "
504:                "VALUES (:path, :name, :purpose, :key_idea, :mtime, :sha256, NOW()) "
505:                "ON CONFLICT (path) DO UPDATE SET "
506:                "  name = EXCLUDED.name, "
507:                "  purpose = EXCLUDED.purpose, "
508:                "  key_idea = EXCLUDED.key_idea, "
509:                "  mtime = EXCLUDED.mtime, "
510:                "  sha256 = EXCLUDED.sha256, "
511:                "  discovered_at = NOW()"
512:            ),
513:            {
514:                "path": rel_path,
515:                "name": name,
516:                "purpose": purpose,
517:                "key_idea": key_idea,
518:                "mtime": mtime,
519:                "sha256": sha256,
520:            },
521:        )
522:        conn.execute(
523:            text("DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_imports WHERE from_path = :p"),
524:            {"p": rel_path},
525:        )
526:        conn.execute(
527:            text("DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_callers WHERE to_path = :p"),
528:            {"p": rel_path},
529:        )
530:        for to_path in imports_list:
531:            conn.execute(
532:                text(
533:                    "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_imports (from_path, to_path) "
534:                    "VALUES (:f, :t) "
535:                    "ON CONFLICT (from_path, to_path) DO NOTHING"
536:                ),
537:                {"f": rel_path, "t": to_path},
538:            )
539:        conn.execute(
540:            text(
541:                "UPDATE codeconv.discover_runs "
542:                "SET files_processed = files_processed + 1 WHERE id = :id"
543:            ),
544:            {"id": run_id},
545:        )
546:
547:    fields = {
548:        "path": rel_path,
549:        "name": name,
550:        "purpose": purpose,
551:        "key_idea": key_idea,
552:        "dependencies": imports_list,
553:        "callers": [],  # filled by _backfill_tombstone_callers
554:        "mtime": _format_mtime(mtime),
555:        "sha256": sha256,
556:    }
557:    # Carry forward the six feature-015 keys if a prior tombstone (e.g. from
558:    # `stamp-tombstones`) had them — discover must not erase depgraph /
559:    # conversion state on re-write (round-trip preservation, Amendment v2).
560:    fields = merge_preserving_feature015(
561:        fields, tombstone_path(tombstones_root, rel_path)
562:    )
563:    write_tombstone(tombstones_root, rel_path, fields)
564:
565:    return "processed"
566:
567:
568:def _backfill_tombstone_callers(
569:    engine: Engine, tombstones_root: Path
570:) -> None:
571:    """Fill the ``callers`` field of every live tombstone from the
572:    finalised ``codeconv.dart_callers`` table.
573:
574:    Live = under ``tombstones_root`` excluding the ``.orphaned/``
575:    subtree. Tombstones are rewritten in place; field ordering and
576:    sorting are enforced by ``write_tombstone``.
577:    """
578:    if not tombstones_root.is_dir():
579:        return
580:    with engine.begin() as conn:
581:        rows = conn.execute(
582:            text(
583:                "SELECT df.path, df.name, df.purpose, df.key_idea, df.mtime, df.sha256 "
584:                "FROM codeconv.dart_files df ORDER BY df.path"
585:            )
586:        ).all()
587:        all_imports = conn.execute(
588:            text(
589:                "SELECT from_path, to_path FROM codeconv.dart_imports"
590:            )
591:        ).all()
592:        all_callers = conn.execute(
593:            text(
594:                "SELECT to_path, from_path FROM codeconv.dart_callers"
595:            )
596:        ).all()
597:
598:    deps_by: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
599:    for f, t in all_imports:
600:        deps_by.setdefault(f, []).append(t)
601:    callers_by: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
602:    for to_path, from_path in all_callers:
603:        callers_by.setdefault(to_path, []).append(from_path)
604:
605:    for path, name, purpose, key_idea, mtime, sha256 in rows:
606:        fields = {
607:            "path": path,
608:            "name": name or Path(path).name,
609:            "purpose": purpose or "",
610:            "key_idea": key_idea or "",
611:            "dependencies": deps_by.get(path, []),
612:            "callers": callers_by.get(path, []),
613:            "mtime": _format_mtime(mtime) if isinstance(mtime, datetime) else str(mtime),
614:            "sha256": sha256,
615:        }
616:        fields = merge_preserving_feature015(
617:            fields, tombstone_path(tombstones_root, path)
618:        )
619:        write_tombstone(tombstones_root, path, fields)
620:
621:
622:def _scan_outside_callers(
623:    repo_root: Path,
624:    subtree: Path,
625:    *,
626:    package_name: Optional[str] = None,
627:) -> list[dict]:
628:    """Scan the repo for ``.dart`` files OUTSIDE the subtree that import
629:    INTO it. Returns a list of warning dicts (FR-023). Edges are NEVER
630:    recorded in ``dart_callers`` for these matches.
631:
632:    Feature 014 / FR-006: when ``package_name`` is supplied, an outside
633:    file importing ``package:<package_name>/...`` triggers the same
634:    ``outside_caller`` warning as the relative-path form.
635:    """
636:    out: list[dict] = []
637:    if not repo_root.is_dir():
638:        return out
639:    subtree_real = subtree.resolve()
640:    lib_real = (subtree_real / "lib").resolve()
641:    self_prefix = (
642:        f"package:{package_name}/" if package_name is not None else None
643:    )
644:    for sibling in repo_root.iterdir():
645:        if not sibling.is_dir():
646:            continue
647:        if sibling.resolve() == subtree_real:
648:            continue
649:        # Skip well-known noise dirs.
650:        if sibling.name in {".git", ".pgdb", ".codeconv", "node_modules", ".venv"}:
651:            continue
652:        for abs_path, _ in walk_dart_files(sibling):
653:            try:
654:                content = abs_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
655:            except OSError:
656:                continue
657:            for m in _IMPORT_RE.finditer(content):
658:                target = m.group("target").strip()
659:
660:                # Feature 014 / FR-006: self-package rewrite parity with
661:                # extract_imports. Must come BEFORE the external-skip.
662:                if (
663:                    self_prefix is not None
664:                    and target.startswith(self_prefix)
665:                    and len(target) > len(self_prefix)
666:                ):
667:                    rest = target[len(self_prefix):]
668:                    try:
669:                        resolved = (subtree_real / "lib" / rest).resolve()
670:                    except (OSError, RuntimeError):
671:                        continue
672:                    try:
673:                        resolved.relative_to(lib_real)
674:                    except ValueError:
675:                        continue
676:                    try:
677:                        inside_rel = resolved.relative_to(
678:                            subtree_real
679:                        ).as_posix()
680:                    except ValueError:
681:                        continue
682:                    try:
683:                        outside_rel = abs_path.resolve().relative_to(
684:                            repo_root
685:                        ).as_posix()
686:                    except ValueError:
687:                        outside_rel = str(abs_path)
688:                    out.append(
689:                        {
690:                            "kind": "outside_caller",
691:                            "outside_file": outside_rel,
692:                            "inside_file": inside_rel,
693:                        }
694:                    )
695:                    continue
696:
697:                if target.startswith(("package:", "dart:", "dart-ext:")):
698:                    continue
699:                try:
700:                    resolved = (abs_path.parent / target).resolve()
701:                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
702:                    continue
703:                try:
704:                    inside_rel = resolved.relative_to(subtree_real).as_posix()
705:                except ValueError:
706:                    continue
707:                try:
708:                    outside_rel = abs_path.resolve().relative_to(repo_root).as_posix()
709:                except ValueError:
710:                    outside_rel = str(abs_path)
711:                out.append(
712:                    {
713:                        "kind": "outside_caller",
714:                        "outside_file": outside_rel,
715:                        "inside_file": inside_rel,
716:                    }
717:                )
718:    return out
719:
720:
721:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
722:# --from-tombstones mode
723:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
724:
725:
726:_REQUIRED_012_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("path", "sha256")
727:
728:# Feature-015 optional keys + their accepted Python types after YAML load.
729:# (``None`` is always accepted — that is the legitimate YAML-null state.)
730:_OPTIONAL_015_TYPES: dict[str, tuple[type, ...]] = {
731:    "topo_level": (int,),
732:    "cycle_group_id": (int,),
733:    "status": (str,),
734:    "conversion_started_at": (str,),
735:    "conversion_completed_at": (str,),
736:    "target_path": (str,),
737:}
738:_STATUS_VALUES = {"pending", "ready", "in_progress", "converted"}
739:
740:
741:def _preflight_from_tombstones(tombstones_root: Path) -> dict:
742:    """Parse + structural-validate + referential-completeness PREFLIGHT
743:    for ``--from-tombstones`` (contract § Steps (--from-tombstones mode)
744:    steps 1–3). Runs BEFORE any bridge / ``discover_runs`` / DB touch.
745:
746:    Returns either ``{"abort": True, "warnings": [...], "error": <str>,
747:    "duration_seconds": ...}`` (caller maps to exit 65, zero mutation) or
748:    a validated payload: ``files`` (list of dart_files row dicts),
749:    ``imports`` / ``callers`` (referentially-complete edge tuples),
750:    ``orphaned`` (dart_files_orphaned row dicts), plus ``warnings``.
751:    """
752:    t0 = time.monotonic()
753:    warnings_list: list[dict] = []
754:    bad_files: list[str] = []
755:    raw: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []  # (rel_path, frontmatter)
756:
757:    if not tombstones_root.is_dir():
758:        return {
759:            "abort": False,
760:            "warnings": warnings_list,
761:            "duration_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2),
762:            "files": [],
763:            "imports": [],
764:            "callers": [],
765:            "orphaned": [],
766:        }
767:
768:    # ---- Step 1+2a: walk T (excl .orphaned/), parse, required-field gate
769:    for tomb_path in sorted(tombstones_root.rglob("*.dart.md")):
770:        rel_to_root = tomb_path.relative_to(tombstones_root)
771:        if ".orphaned" in rel_to_root.parts:
772:            continue
773:        try:
774:            fm = read_tombstone(tomb_path)
775:        except Exception as exc:  # unparseable → hard-fail
776:            bad_files.append(f"{tomb_path} ({exc})")
777:            continue
778:        missing = []
779:        for key in _REQUIRED_012_FIELDS:
780:            val = fm.get(key)
781:            if not isinstance(val, str) or not val.strip():
782:                missing.append(key)
783:        if missing:
784:            bad_files.append(
785:                f"{tomb_path} (missing/invalid required field(s): "
786:                f"{', '.join(missing)})"
787:            )
788:            continue
789:        raw.append((str(fm["path"]).replace("\\", "/"), fm))
790:
791:    if bad_files:
792:        return {
793:            "abort": True,
794:            "warnings": warnings_list,
795:            "error": (
796:                "codeconv discover --from-tombstones: ABORT — "
797:                f"{len(bad_files)} malformed / required-field-invalid "
798:                "tombstone(s); inventory left untouched:\n  "
799:                + "\n  ".join(bad_files)
800:            ),
801:            "duration_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2),
802:        }
803:
804:    # ---- Step 2b: optional feature-015 key type validation (warn-only) --
805:    for rel_path, fm in raw:
806:        for key, types in _OPTIONAL_015_TYPES.items():
807:            if key not in fm:
808:                continue
809:            val = fm[key]
810:            if val is None:
811:                continue  # legitimate YAML-null
812:            ok = isinstance(val, types)
813:            if ok and key == "status" and val not in _STATUS_VALUES:
814:                ok = False
815:            if not ok:
816:                warnings_list.append(
817:                    {
818:                        "kind": "type_invalid_015_field",
819:                        "path": rel_path,
820:                        "field": key,
821:                    }
822:                )
823:
824:    valid_paths = {rel_path for rel_path, _ in raw}
825:
826:    # ---- Step 3: referential completeness (drop+warn dangling edges) ----
827:    files: list[dict] = []
828:    imports: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
829:    callers: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
830:    for rel_path, fm in raw:
831:        files.append(
832:            {
833:                "path": rel_path,
834:                "name": fm.get("name") or Path(rel_path).name,
835:                "purpose": fm.get("purpose") or "",
836:                "key_idea": fm.get("key_idea") or "",
837:                "mtime": fm.get("mtime") or _format_mtime(_utc_now()),
838:                "sha256": fm["sha256"],
839:            }
840:        )
841:        deps = fm.get("dependencies")
842:        if deps is not None and not isinstance(deps, list):
843:            warnings_list.append(
844:                {"kind": "malformed_field", "path": rel_path, "field": "dependencies"}
845:            )
846:            deps = []
847:        for dep in deps or []:
848:            dep = str(dep).replace("\\", "/")
849:            if dep not in valid_paths:
850:                warnings_list.append(
851:                    {
852:                        "kind": "missing_tombstone",
853:                        "path": dep,
854:                        "referrer": rel_path,
855:                    }
856:                )
857:                continue
858:            imports.append((rel_path, dep))
859:        clrs = fm.get("callers")
860:        if clrs is not None and not isinstance(clrs, list):
861:            warnings_list.append(
862:                {"kind": "malformed_field", "path": rel_path, "field": "callers"}
863:            )
864:            clrs = []
865:        for caller in clrs or []:
866:            caller = str(caller).replace("\\", "/")
867:            if caller not in valid_paths:
868:                warnings_list.append(
869:                    {
870:                        "kind": "missing_tombstone",
871:                        "path": caller,
872:                        "referrer": rel_path,
873:                    }
874:                )
875:                continue
876:            callers.append((caller, rel_path))
877:
878:    # ---- Collect orphaned tombstones for the step-6 UPSERT --------------
879:    orphaned: list[dict] = []
880:    orphan_root = tombstones_root / ".orphaned"
881:    if orphan_root.is_dir():
882:        for tomb_path in sorted(orphan_root.rglob("*.dart.md")):
883:            try:
884:                fm = read_tombstone(tomb_path)
885:            except Exception as exc:
886:                warnings_list.append(
887:                    {
888:                        "kind": "malformed_orphan_tombstone",
889:                        "path": str(tomb_path),
890:                        "error": str(exc),
891:                    }
892:                )
893:                continue
894:            opath = fm.get("path")
895:            if not isinstance(opath, str) or not opath.strip():
896:                continue
897:            orphaned.append(
898:                {
899:                    "path": opath.replace("\\", "/"),
900:                    "name": fm.get("name") or Path(opath).name,
901:                    "purpose": fm.get("purpose") or "",
902:                    "key_idea": fm.get("key_idea") or "",
903:                    "mtime": fm.get("mtime") or _format_mtime(_utc_now()),
904:                    "sha256": fm.get("sha256") or "",
905:                }
906:            )
907:
908:    return {
909:        "abort": False,
910:        "warnings": warnings_list,
911:        "duration_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2),
912:        "files": files,
913:        "imports": imports,
914:        "callers": callers,
915:        "orphaned": orphaned,
916:    }
917:
918:
919:def _run_from_tombstones(
920:    engine: Engine,
921:    run_id: str,
922:    preflight: dict,
923:    dry_run: bool,
924:) -> dict:
925:    """Apply a validated ``--from-tombstones`` preflight payload to the
926:    inventory in ONE transaction (contract § Steps step 5 — Option B).
927:
928:    dart_imports / dart_callers are TRUNCATEd then bulk-reinserted (no
929:    inbound FK). dart_files is UPSERTed per surviving path then only the
930:    paths absent from T are DELETEd — so feature-015's dart_conversions /
931:    dart_depgraph (FK → dart_files ON DELETE CASCADE) are PRESERVED for
932:    every surviving file. A path genuinely absent from T is a vanished
933:    file; its DELETE cascades its conversion/depgraph row away.
934:    """
935:    t0 = time.monotonic()
936:    warnings_list: list[dict] = list(preflight["warnings"])
937:    files: list[dict] = preflight["files"]
938:    imports: list[tuple[str, str]] = preflight["imports"]
939:    callers: list[tuple[str, str]] = preflight["callers"]
940:    orphaned: list[dict] = preflight["orphaned"]
941:
942:    if dry_run:
943:        return {
944:            "files_walked": len(files),
945:            "files_processed": len(files),
946:            "files_skipped_idempotent": 0,
947:            "imports": len(imports),
948:            "callers": len(callers),
949:            "orphaned": len(orphaned),
950:            "revived": 0,
951:            "warnings": warnings_list,
952:            "duration_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2),
953:        }
954:
955:    keep_paths = [f["path"] for f in files]
956:
957:    with engine.begin() as conn:  # ONE transaction (atomic-per-run)
958:        # dart_imports / dart_callers: no inbound FK → safe to TRUNCATE.
959:        conn.execute(
960:            text(
961:                "TRUNCATE codeconv.dart_imports, codeconv.dart_callers"
962:            )
963:        )
964:        # dart_files: UPSERT each surviving path (NOT a blanket TRUNCATE,
965:        # so dart_conversions / dart_depgraph survive via the FK).
966:        for f in files:
967:            conn.execute(
968:                text(
969:                    "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_files "
970:                    "  (path, name, purpose, key_idea, mtime, sha256, discovered_at) "
971:                    "VALUES (:path, :name, :purpose, :key_idea, "
972:                    "        :mtime, :sha256, NOW()) "
973:                    "ON CONFLICT (path) DO UPDATE SET "
974:                    "  name = EXCLUDED.name, "
975:                    "  purpose = EXCLUDED.purpose, "
976:                    "  key_idea = EXCLUDED.key_idea, "
977:                    "  mtime = EXCLUDED.mtime, "
978:                    "  sha256 = EXCLUDED.sha256, "
979:                    "  discovered_at = NOW()"
980:                ),
981:                f,
982:            )
983:        # DELETE only vanished paths → cascades their conversion/depgraph.
984:        if keep_paths:
985:            stmt = text(
986:                "DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_files WHERE path NOT IN :keep"
987:            ).bindparams(bindparam("keep", expanding=True))
988:            conn.execute(stmt, {"keep": keep_paths})
989:        else:
990:            conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM codeconv.dart_files"))
991:        # Reinsert the referentially-complete edge sets.
992:        for f_path, t_path in imports:
993:            conn.execute(
994:                text(
995:                    "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_imports (from_path, to_path) "
996:                    "VALUES (:f, :t) "
997:                    "ON CONFLICT (from_path, to_path) DO NOTHING"
998:                ),
999:                {"f": f_path, "t": t_path},
1000:            )
1001:        for f_path, t_path in callers:
1002:            conn.execute(
1003:                text(
1004:                    "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_callers (from_path, to_path) "
1005:                    "VALUES (:f, :t) "
1006:                    "ON CONFLICT (from_path, to_path) DO NOTHING"
1007:                ),
1008:                {"f": f_path, "t": t_path},
1009:            )
1010:        # Step 6: UPSERT .orphaned/ tombstones into dart_files_orphaned.
1011:        for o in orphaned:
1012:            conn.execute(
1013:                text(
1014:                    "INSERT INTO codeconv.dart_files_orphaned "
1015:                    "  (path, name, purpose, key_idea, mtime, sha256, "
1016:                    "   discovered_at, orphaned_at) "
1017:                    "VALUES (:path, :name, :purpose, :key_idea, "
1018:                    "        :mtime, :sha256, NOW(), NOW()) "
1019:                    "ON CONFLICT (path) DO UPDATE SET "
1020:                    "  name = EXCLUDED.name, "
1021:                    "  purpose = EXCLUDED.purpose, "
1022:                    "  key_idea = EXCLUDED.key_idea, "
1023:                    "  mtime = EXCLUDED.mtime, "
1024:                    "  sha256 = EXCLUDED.sha256, "
1025:                    "  orphaned_at = NOW()"
1026:                ),
1027:                o,
1028:            )
1029:
1030:    with engine.begin() as conn:
1031:        imports_count = (
1032:            conn.execute(
1033:                text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM codeconv.dart_imports")
1034:            ).scalar()
1035:            or 0
1036:        )
1037:        callers_count = (
1038:            conn.execute(
1039:                text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM codeconv.dart_callers")
1040:            ).scalar()
1041:            or 0
1042:        )
1043:
1044:    return {
1045:        "files_walked": len(files),
1046:        "files_processed": len(files),
1047:        "files_skipped_idempotent": 0,
1048:        "imports": int(imports_count),
1049:        "callers": int(callers_count),
1050:        "orphaned": len(orphaned),
1051:        "revived": 0,
1052:        "warnings": warnings_list,
1053:        "duration_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2),
1054:    }
1055:
1056:
1057:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1058:# --verify-tombstones mode (read-only source-truth audit; NO bridge)
1059:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1060:
1061:
1062:def _run_verify_tombstones(
1063:    repo_root: Path,
1064:    subtree: Path,
1065:    tombstones_root: Path,
1066:) -> dict:
1067:    """Read-only source-truth audit (contract § Steps (--verify-tombstones
1068:    mode)). Reads ``.dart`` sources; NO DB writes, NO tombstone rewrites;
1069:    bridge NOT acquired. Returns a discover-shaped summary with the four
1070:    verify counts + an ``exit_code`` (0 / 1 / 65).
1071:    """
1072:    t0 = time.monotonic()
1073:    warnings_list: list[dict] = []
1074:
1075:    def _summary(
1076:        *,
1077:        exit_code: int,
1078:        error: Optional[str] = None,
1079:        verified_clean: int = 0,
1080:        stale: int = 0,
1081:        missing_source: int = 0,
1082:        missing_tombstone: int = 0,
1083:        walked: int = 0,
1084:    ) -> dict:
1085:        out: dict = {
1086:            "files_walked": walked,
1087:            "files_processed": verified_clean,
1088:            "files_skipped_idempotent": 0,
1089:            "imports": 0,
1090:            "callers": 0,
1091:            "orphaned": 0,
1092:            "revived": 0,
1093:            "verified_clean": verified_clean,
1094:            "stale": stale,
1095:            "missing_source": missing_source,
1096:            "missing_tombstone": missing_tombstone,
1097:            "warnings": warnings_list,
1098:            "duration_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2),
1099:            "exit_code": exit_code,
1100:        }
1101:        if error is not None:
1102:            out["error"] = error
1103:        return out
1104:
1105:    # ---- Step 1: walk T (excl .orphaned/) ------------------------------
1106:    tomb_set: list[tuple[Path, dict]] = []
1107:    bad_files: list[str] = []
1108:    if tombstones_root.is_dir():
1109:        for tomb_path in sorted(tombstones_root.rglob("*.dart.md")):
1110:            rel_to_root = tomb_path.relative_to(tombstones_root)
1111:            if ".orphaned" in rel_to_root.parts:
1112:                continue
1113:            try:
1114:                fm = read_tombstone(tomb_path)
1115:            except Exception as exc:
1116:                bad_files.append(f"{tomb_path} ({exc})")
1117:                continue
1118:            # Verify-mode is a source-truth audit: a format-invalid
1119:            # tombstone (missing/!type-valid REQUIRED 012 field — path
1120:            # AND sha256) aborts exit 65, same gate as the
1121:            # --from-tombstones preflight. sha256 is required for the
1122:            # recompute-and-compare; without it the file can't be
1123:            # audited and MUST NOT pass as a mere stale warning.
1124:            missing = [
1125:                k
1126:                for k in _REQUIRED_012_FIELDS
1127:                if not isinstance(fm.get(k), str) or not fm[k].strip()
1128:            ]
1129:            if missing:
1130:                bad_files.append(
1131:                    f"{tomb_path} (missing/invalid required field(s): "
1132:                    f"{', '.join(missing)})"
1133:                )
1134:                continue
1135:            tomb_set.append((tomb_path, fm))
1136:
1137:    # ---- Step 2: unparseable / format-invalid → abort exit 65 ----------
1138:    if bad_files:
1139:        return _summary(
1140:            exit_code=65,
1141:            error=(
1142:                "codeconv discover --verify-tombstones: ABORT — "
1143:                f"{len(bad_files)} malformed / unparseable tombstone(s):\n  "
1144:                + "\n  ".join(bad_files)
1145:            ),
1146:        )
1147:
1148:    # ---- Step 3: build whole-subtree import graph ONCE -----------------
1149:    package_name, _ = read_package_name(subtree, repo_root=repo_root)
1150:    present: dict[str, Path] = {}
1151:    for abs_path, rel in walk_dart_files(subtree):
1152:        present[rel.replace("\\", "/")] = abs_path
1153:
1154:    # Step 5: zero .dart sources under the subtree → exit 1.
1155:    if not present:
1156:        return _summary(
1157:            exit_code=1,
1158:            error=(
1159:                "codeconv discover --verify-tombstones: no .dart sources "
1160:                f"under {subtree} — source-truth verification needs sources "
1161:                "(exit 1; not 2)."
1162:            ),
1163:            walked=len(tomb_set),
1164:        )
1165:
1166:    derived_deps: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
1167:    derived_sha: dict[str, str] = {}
1168:    for rel, abs_path in present.items():
1169:        derived_deps[rel] = sorted(
1170:            extract_imports(abs_path, subtree, package_name)
1171:        )
1172:        try:
1173:            derived_sha[rel] = hashlib.sha256(
1174:                abs_path.read_bytes()
1175:            ).hexdigest()
1176:        except OSError:
1177:            derived_sha[rel] = ""
1178:    derived_callers: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
1179:    for frm, tos in derived_deps.items():
1180:        for to in tos:
1181:            derived_callers.setdefault(to, []).append(frm)
1182:    for k in derived_callers:
1183:        derived_callers[k] = sorted(set(derived_callers[k]))
1184:
1185:    tomb_paths = {
1186:        str(fm["path"]).replace("\\", "/") for _, fm in tomb_set
1187:    }
1188:
1189:    verified_clean = stale = missing_source = 0
1190:    for _tomb_path, fm in tomb_set:
1191:        rel_path = str(fm["path"]).replace("\\", "/")
1192:        if rel_path not in present:
1193:            warnings_list.append(
1194:                {"kind": "missing_source", "path": rel_path}
1195:            )
1196:            missing_source += 1
1197:            continue
1198:        diff_fields: list[str] = []
1199:        if str(fm.get("sha256") or "") != derived_sha.get(rel_path, ""):
1200:            diff_fields.append("sha256")
1201:        rec_deps = sorted(
1202:            str(d).replace("\\", "/") for d in (fm.get("dependencies") or [])
1203:        )
1204:        if rec_deps != derived_deps.get(rel_path, []):
1205:            diff_fields.append("dependencies")
1206:        rec_callers = sorted(
1207:            str(c).replace("\\", "/") for c in (fm.get("callers") or [])
1208:        )
1209:        if rec_callers != derived_callers.get(rel_path, []):
1210:            diff_fields.append("callers")
1211:        if diff_fields:
1212:            warnings_list.append(
1213:                {
1214:                    "kind": "stale_tombstone",
1215:                    "path": rel_path,
1216:                    "fields": diff_fields,
1217:                }
1218:            )
1219:            stale += 1
1220:        else:
1221:            verified_clean += 1
1222:
1223:    # ---- Step 4: .dart with no tombstone in T → missing_tombstone ------
1224:    missing_tombstone = 0
1225:    for rel in sorted(present):
1226:        if rel not in tomb_paths:
1227:            warnings_list.append(
1228:                {"kind": "missing_tombstone", "path": rel}
1229:            )
1230:            missing_tombstone += 1
1231:
1232:    return _summary(
1233:        exit_code=0,
1234:        verified_clean=verified_clean,
1235:        stale=stale,
1236:        missing_source=missing_source,
1237:        missing_tombstone=missing_tombstone,
1238:        walked=len(tomb_set),
1239:    )
1240:
1241:
1242:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1243:# Helpers
1244:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
1245:
1246:
1247:def _utc_now() -> datetime:
1248:    return datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc)
1249:
1250:
1251:def _format_mtime(dt: datetime) -> str:
1252:    """ISO-8601 UTC with ms precision (matches PG ``timestamptz`` text)."""
1253:    if dt.tzinfo is None:
1254:        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
1255:    ms = dt.microsecond // 1000
1256:    return dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.") + f"{ms:03d}Z"

534:                    "VALUES (:f, :t) "
535:                    "ON CONFLICT (from_path, to_path) DO NOTHING"
536:                ),
537:                {"f": rel_path, "t": to_path},
538:            )
539:        conn.execute(
540:            text(

542:                "SET files_processed = files_processed + 1 WHERE id = :id"
543:            ),
544:            {"id": run_id},

549:        "name": name,
550:        "purpose": purpose,
551:        "key_idea": key_idea,

553:        "callers": [],  # filled by _backfill_tombstone_callers

558:    # `stamp-tombstones`) had them — discover must not erase depgraph /

584:                "FROM codeconv.dart_files df ORDER BY df.path"

32:_IMPORT_RE = re.compile(
33:    r"""^\s*import\s+(?P<q>['"])(?P<target>[^'"]+)(?P=q)\s*(?:show|hide|as|;)""",
34:    re.MULTILINE,
